Consumes `Page`, `PageHead`, `Scroller`, `Columns`, `Sentence`, `MessageRow`, `SectionHead`, `Ruled`, `RunHost`, `ask`.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **A fact.** `Fact(kind, sentence, blocks="")`; `LEADS = {"err": "Error.", "lock": "Blocked.", "warn": "Attention.", "info": "Information.", "fine": "Fine."}`. An "err" or "lock" fact holds its `blocks` words, nothing else does; `Fact.__post_init__` raises `ValueError` on a fact that breaks this. `ordered(facts)`: blocking first, each group in the order handed in.
- **`reason_beside`:** live: "A run is live. Only one runs at a time."; one blocking fact: "Cannot start yet: the name cannot be used."; several: "Cannot start yet: the name cannot be used, and 2 more above."; `nothing_to_apply`: "Nothing to apply: these choices are in use."; else "". "1 more" for two blocking facts.
- **`origin_words`** holds 7.1.8's table: `held` (the source holds the setting) wins; else `kept`; else usual. `source` "plan", "record" or "run"; `name` the source's. "from plan retrain", "changed from the plan", "usual", "changed from the usual", "kept from last time".
- **`about_words`:** 30 "about half a minute"; 60 "about a minute"; 92 "about a minute and a half"; 150 "about 2 and a half minutes"; 600 "about 10 minutes"; 3600 "about an hour"; 5400 "about an hour and a half"; rounded to the half minute below ten minutes, to the minute below an hour, to the half hour above.
- **The form.** A grid row: label column `design.SIZE["label_w"]`, field, unit, origin in the small type; the line under the field; the row gap the look gives. Each label is its field's buddy. `form.rows` holds main and folded rows alike. Two columns from `design.two_columns_from()` through `Columns`, `fit` forwarded. Rebuilt rows keep `HelpBadge` beside the label as a hover tip (departure 135), never a Tab stop. A number field: `IntField(grouped=True)` or `FloatField`, right aligned, equal-width digits, `design.SIZE["number_w"]`.
- **The fold.** `FOLD_WORDS = "Rarely changed ({count})"`, `FOLD_NOT_USUAL = "Rarely changed ({count}, {m} not usual)"`, `count` being "1 setting" or "N settings", made by the caller. Folded at every launch. While folded, one small sentence of `label value` pairs, those not usual first, `; ` between them. A folded field in error opens the fold, and it cannot be closed until the error is gone. `QPushButton[cls="fold"]`, as wide as its words; a `Ruled` line above and below; a painted chevron, `@reported(QWidget.paintEvent)`, right when folded and down when open, tested in `tests/test_look.py` under its rules.
- **Name and note (5.5):** `QLineEdit` both (the Note is one line, so row 267.2 needs no rule). The name: `store.assert_name_free(kind, text)` 300 ms after the last keystroke by a stopped single-shot timer, and by `check_now()` at the press; never while `controller().is_live()`: an edit pending at a run's start is dropped, and read at its end. Passes: "Fine. This name is free." beside the field. Refused: outline, the engine's sentence under it, `Fact("err", <sentence>, "the name cannot be used")`. Blank and required: `Fact("err", "Type a name.", "a name is needed")`. The note: `require_note("note", text)` at every keystroke, its sentence likewise, blocks "the note cannot be used". `clear()` empties both and drops the hint; `set_hint` shows a quiet line under "Name".
- **`Facts` and `Foot`.** Rows are kept across a reading: `Facts.set_facts` reuses a row whose fact is unchanged and calls `MessageRow.set_kind`, `set_sentence` otherwise, as `RunView._set_messages` does. Height: what the rows need, at most `design.scaled(FACTS_CAP)`, past which they scroll inside their own `Scroller` (departure 150). Small layout (`set_small(True)`): only `err`, `lock`, `warn` facts, with the link "Show all 5" / "Show fewer", opening inside the same region; with nothing hidden, no link. The foot's line: the reason (`Sentence`), "Check again", the main button, primary, at the right. The reason is also the button's accessible description. While a run is live the reason gains the link "Go to the run" (`Foot.go_to_run`, connected through `InferenceScreen.go_to_run` to `MainWindow.go_to_run`). `Foot.set_live(True)` disables the two buttons; facts stay enabled and readable.
- **`StepPage`.** Layout: `self.page = Page()` in a zero-margin box. `head = PageHead(title, sentence)`, with the plain button "Help" (opens Help at its top, through `navigate`). Gap as Home's. `self.results = QStackedWidget()` through `add_stack`: `self.figures` (a column: a `Scroller` over [`in_use`, the how-link, `figure_stack`, the next-step row, `form`], then `foot`), `run_view`, the past view when asked. `figure_stack` is hidden while it holds nothing. `_run_began`: `figure_stack.clear_all()` first, unless the run only loads. `_figures_leaving` keeps nothing; `_figures_back` re-inserts the stack at its place. `controls` is `form`: `set_controls_enabled(on)` is `controls.setEnabled(on)`, `foot.set_live(not on)`, then, on `True`, `refresh_local_gates()`. `first_focus()` answers None while the form is locked, so a step first opened during a run leaves the keyboard on its rail row. `refresh_local_gates()` runs `in_use`, the selection rows and `redraw()`, and reads nothing. `save_settings` does nothing; `restore_settings(qs)` stands for the pages. A page enrols through `_enrol()`.
- **When facts are read (5.4):** `read_facts()`: live: the reading line as below, nothing read. Else `read_disk()`, the time kept, `redraw()`. Triggers: `showEvent` (once per staleness), `read_timer` after an edit to something it depends on, a bound method on `controller().run_ended`, `InferenceScreen.stale_store()`, "Check again", and at the press. A start taken back sends no `run_ended`, and `_take_back` is not edited, so a bound method on `status_changed` calls `read_soon()` at a change to not live; `read_facts()` stops the timer, so a run's end still reads once. A page not shown marks `stale`. `refresh_local_gates` is no trigger. Lines: "The saved records were read at 12:04.", "A run is live. Read at 12:04; read again when it ends.", "A run is live. Not read yet; read when it ends.".
- **`fix_sentence(key, here=None)`:** with `here` None, the word-for-word sentences of today, which the old pages keep. With `here` a step or part: a place the row stands on gives "Set it in 'Batches' on this page."; another gives "Set it in 'Batches' in step 3, Training, or step 6, Narrowing round."; a sentence entry gives `ON_THIS_PAGE[key]` when it has one, else itself. `ON_THIS_PAGE`: `new_run` "Answer the question that the start button asks on this page."; `name` "Choose another name in 'Name' on this page."; `note` "Change it in 'Note' on this page.". `controller._refusal_words` takes the run's spec and passes `here=spec.place[1]` for a rebuilt page (a host with a `step`), so a refusal on its run page says the same. `_FIXED` changes with its page (Task 8).
- **Stage-1 rows.** `labeled_inputs.reading(field, key) -> str | None`: the field's own sentence first (`field.error()`), then "… is blank." (637.1). A rewrite inside `_edited` leaves `textChanged` heard twice; a listener reads `field.value_or_none()`, never the signal's text (637.3, a one-line note and the test). `set_error` keeps a tool tip set while in error (637.9). `_whole(None)` is None (647.1). The comma's sentence stands while the comma is there (647.3), kept by the row and not by the field (departure 200). `QPushButton[cls="link"]:disabled` and disabled `small`, `quiet`, `book2` labels take `disabled_ink` (259.6, 259.7). A `Choice` is at least as wide as its longest item, chevron included (637.10); one test of its own here, and the model list's (Task 8), the file list's (Task 9) and the plan list's (Task 12) stand on it.

Folded: "Driven recordings per observation", "Slots for driven recordings", "Lock-in limit" (unit "drive cycles"), each shown only for the frequency response; "Rotation" (`rot_choice`, "On", "Off"); "Units" (`units_choice`: "From the model's units file", "Typed here"; `units_text`). `CONTROL` takes these labels; `chi_mode` and `reparam_rotate` get their rows. `plan_fields()` holds `chi_mode`, `chi_n_freqs`, `chi_k_pad`, `chi_max_cycles`, `reparam_rotate`, `units`. The two measured values are `add_text` rows: "Drive amplitude in training: 0.15 (no unit)." and "Frequency band in training: 0.03 to 0.3 times the cell's own frequency.", read from `config.CHI_F0`, `config.CHI_FREQ_BOUNDS`, origin "fixed by measurement". `_FIXED` becomes "Fixed by measurement: it cannot be changed in the window."
- **The model list.** `class _ModelChoice(Choice)` holds each engine name as item data and shows `chain.model_label(name)`; `addItems(names)`, `currentText()` and `setCurrentText(name)` speak engine names, so `MainWindow._refresh_model_combos` and every test stand. `currentIndexChanged` drives `_on_model_changed`.
- **The sentences:** 8.2's four explanations, word for word, each under its setting, and "Typed units cannot continue a simulation set made with the model's units file, even when they match it." under "Typed here". The units line: "kHz ms nm pN, from Units/nadrowski/units.txt": the words sorted, the path from `cli.resolve_units_file` relative to `config.RESOURCES_ROOT`. `units_text` is refilled from the selected model's file whenever the model changes.
- **Rule table.** One table read by the live check and the click, as today's rules at `config_tab.py:230-260`: 2 ≤ per observation ≤ slots ≤ 24; lock-in limit above 2; typed units must resolve. Its lines under the fields are built from the row's label.
- **`_build_config()`:** read through the table (a refusal changes nothing); `ask(self, "Start a new session?", "Applying releases what this session holds: the prior retrain_prior; the posterior retrain. A different model, units or kind of observation makes them invalid. Each stays in Records and can be loaded again.", "Keep this session", "Apply and start a new session")` when `session_contents()` is not empty; `new_draft(draft)`. `new_draft` then writes `inference_config/model` and `inference_config/chi_mode` through `put_many`, in the one place.
- **Main button:** "Apply model and options" (no `&` of its own; `Foot.set_main_words` gives the access key). A model of your own that inference cannot take: `Fact("err", "This model has external forcing, so parameter inference cannot use it.", "this model cannot be used")`, or "This model has no free parameters to infer, so parameter inference cannot use it." `nothing_to_apply()`: a draft is held and no selection row reads changed.
- **In use.** `model_in_use(draft, units_words)` -> `("Nadrowski", "Frequency response, 6 driven recordings in 12 slots, lock-in limit 20 drive cycles. Rotation on. Units kHz ms nm pN, from the model's file.")`, or "Set by the bounds file. Rotation on. Units …". Rotation always stated. "In use: nothing yet." with "Choose the model and the kind of observation, then apply them." Next step, after an apply: the plain button "Go to Prior", through `navigate`.
- **Selection rows:** every row of the step; the line reads "Changed, not applied. In use: Nadrowski."; `in_use.set_pending(n)`: "1 choice below is changed and not applied.", "2 choices below are changed and not applied." `SelectionRow.changed()` is False while nothing is in use and for an in-use value a stand-in does not hold (read through `getattr`, a missing name counts as unchanged). `refresh_local_gates`, `in_use_words` and `read_disk` never raise on what the session holds.
- **The chain.** `recompute_states` passes `pending=self.pending()`; `pending()` asks each page that has `selection_rows`. `StepState.pending` is True on such a row, and `step_in_hand` passes over it, so Home's next step reads on from what is held, as 3.6's table has it.
- **Remembered:** `restore_settings` puts back the model and the kind, marked "kept from last time" until touched, and reads nothing else. A stale `units_mode`, `units_text`, `reparam_rotate`, `layout/ConfigPanel/…` is neither read nor rewritten. `save_settings` does nothing.
- **Gone:** the session line, the two text-pane paragraphs at `:343-360`, the tick boxes, `_ChiRangeRow`'s use. A rebuilt page holds no `QMessageBox`, no `log_pane`.

**Interfaces:** Produces `PriorPanel(StepPage)`, `show_view(view: str)` ("saved", "build"), `load_record(ref) -> bool`, `InferenceScreen.take_prior(cfg, loaded)`, `prior_facts(...)`, `bounds_in_use(cfg) -> str | None` (the picker's form of `cfg.sources["bounds"]`; "" for typed limits), `ArtifactPicker(..., keeps_selection=False, hint="")`, `StorePicker(..., keeps_selection=False)`, `BoundsGrid(parent=None, *, scrolls=True)`. Keeps `bounds_picker`, `prior_picker`, `_on_prior` (`tests/test_nav_and_gating.py:618`, `:4662`, `tests/test_worker_dispatch.py:406`, `tests/test_artifact_browser.py:1099`). Consumes `history.duration`, `about_words`, `file_manager.parse_bounds_file`, `provenance.file_ref`, `training_checkpoint.checkpoints_using_prior`.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **Two views,** one main button each. Heading's plain button: "Build a new prior instead…" / "Use a saved prior instead…". The page opens on the saved view when a saved prior exists (read at the first `showEvent`, never in the constructor). Rows: "Bounds file" (M, both views), "Saved prior" (M, saved view), "Name", "Note" (build view, `NameAndNote("prior", required=True)`), folded in the build view: "Edit the limits by hand" (a tick; the grid under it), "Sweep rounds", "Candidates per round" (`TickedField(IntField(...), "Automatic", 0)`), "Stable sets to collect", "Random-walk step", "Stability test length" (unit "model time units"), "Smallest cluster", "Cluster density". `_KNOB_RULES` stays the one table. `CONTROL` takes these labels, the `name` sentence reworded ("Choose another name in 'Name' in step 2, Prior, or step 3, Training, or in the 'Record name' or 'Comparison name' box in …").
- **`take_prior(cfg, loaded)`,** called by `_on_prior` while the run is live: `s.reset_downstream("prior")`, `s.inf_prior = loaded`, `_fan_out(cfg)` (`s.cfg = cfg`, `infer_panel.on_config_built(cfg)`), `put_many([("inference_prior/bounds", …), ("inference_prior/prior", loaded.id)])`, then `refresh_gates()` once. The picker is re-listed and moved at the page's `run_ended`, not here. `install_config(cfg)` becomes `_fan_out(cfg)` then `refresh_gates()`. `cfg` is `self._candidate`, the object the click built and handed the run. A page's disk facts wait for `run_ended`.
- **The candidate** comes from `draft.make_config(path)` or `make_config(bounds_dicts=…)`, built once per selection for the facts (cached by path and modification time) and again at the click. A malformed file is `Fact("err", …, "the bounds file cannot be read")` beside the list, inside the guard (W049).
- **The question,** only with a posterior held: titles "Load this prior?", "Build a new prior?"; sentence "This session holds the posterior retrain and the observation cell_a. They belong to the prior in use, so another prior releases them once it has loaded. Each stays in Records and can be loaded again." (for a build "once it is built"; with no observation, the posterior alone); "Keep the posterior"; "Load and release the posterior", "Build and release the posterior".
- **What the file is:** under the list: "13 parameters: 10 of the model and 3 scales (x_scale, t_scale, T)", then the names; "It has a forcing section." or "It has no forcing section."; "With the kind of observation applied in step 1, a posterior trained on it reads: a frequency response.", or "a spontaneous recording and one driven recording", "a spontaneous recording only", read from `candidate.observation_mode`, never a copy of its rule.
- **Limits typed by hand:** `BoundsGrid(scrolls=False)`: no nested area, the page scrolls, and the grid keeps room at its foot for the ring. The grid is reloaded only when another bounds file is selected or step 1 is applied. Under it: "Typed limits are not saved as a file. Records made from them name no bounds file." Build order is Tab order: the grid's fields follow the tick, not the page's end. With the tick off, or the fold shut, no Tab stop lands in the grid (666.2), and the grid joins `test_tab_walks_the_page_in_view_at_every_size`. `problems()` become blocking facts and marks.
- **`prior_facts(*, blocked, built_on, selected, same_box, edited, sets_of_prior, largest, candidates, rounds, batch, stable, steps, took, name_facts, field_facts, sets_for_model, plan_lines=())`,** the rows of 8.3's table, each sentence exact, with its three rules: "…which these two files share, so the load goes through." only when the record's `body["gmm"]` equals the selected file's, else "…which differ between these two files, so PRISM will refuse the load."; the steps of the cost sentence only when the configuration in use was built on the selected file; with no earlier build, "No prior has been built on this computer yet, so the time is not known."
- **The button's words:** "Build a new prior (about a minute and a half)", from `about_words` on `history.duration(folder, "Prior build")`, the fact's length written "1 min 32 s"; the bracket left out when none. "Load this prior": `dispatch(orchestrator.build_prior, cfg, id, False, on_result=self._on_prior)`; the build with `name=`, `note=`, the seven settings, `if_name_taken="unnamed"`, `provide_fig_sink=True`. Run spec as Task 7 of stage 3 gave it, with `name`.
- **Saved prior's facts,** read from `default_store().get("prior", id)`: name, when it was made, model, parameters, bounds file. A prior of another model: `Fact("err", "This prior was made for the model BP.", "this prior is for another model")`.
- **Afterwards:** "In use: retrain_prior" with "Built on master_tier1.txt, 13 parameters. Saved as a record."; the figure; "Go to Training". The units messages of `cfg.check_unit_consistency()` and the force-scale sentence stand as "Attention" facts while that configuration is in use. A saved list re-read keeps its selection. Name and note cleared after a run that made its record, kept after any other end. A record back under another name: `in_use.say(Fact("warn", "It was saved without its name: 'retrain' was taken while the run was going."))`; "Rename…" is stage 6's.
- **Pickers (opt-in, all else as today):** with `keeps_selection=True`, `refresh` re-selects the key while it exists, else no selection and the placeholder ("Choose a bounds file"), never row one; signals are held during a re-list, so no entry is judged in passing; `repoint(base, key)` selects the pick in use, not the one stashed at launch. A remembered file gone: "The bounds file used last time, master_tier1.txt, is no longer in the folder." `StorePicker` here takes no `allow_new`. With `keeps_selection=True` the combo is a `Choice`, so it takes Task 7's width (637.10). `tests/test_settings_persistence.py:517` stands for the Reduction page.
- **A load's failure (departure 151):** `_on_error` on `StepPage`: `ask(self, "Error", message, "OK", marker="err", detail=<type and traceback>)`; a refusal goes to `_refusal` (5.7).
- **Store changes:** `MainWindow._store_changed` calls `inference.stale_store()` after `refresh_gates`.

Left blank, PRISM takes the epochs of the last finished training of this kind in Records."; "No earlier training of this kind: the run's page will say only when the fit cannot end before."; with "Never" (departure 130): "No simulation set is kept or continued. A stop or a failure during simulation loses every simulation; a stop or a failure during the fit loses the fit, which cannot be tried again without simulating again; and the check 'Use of each part of the input' cannot be run on the posterior." With "Never", `resume_choice` is fixed at its first choice and greyed with that reason. The rotation rows: read-only with "Not used: this training reuses the saved simulation set's rotation." or "Not used: rotation is off.", not judged, not passed.
- **`read_disk()`:** `orchestrator.training_reading(cfg, prior, num_runs=, run_size_cap=, checkpoint_every=)`; with "Never", once more at `row.usual()`; `history.estimate` per batch and per epoch; `controller().expected_epochs(spec)`; `history.last_on`; the kept matches for Task 11. Blank or out-of-rule budget boxes: no reading, the field's fact stands. A page built during a run reads nothing.
- **`training_facts(*, blocked, uses, budget, reading, never_reading, never, resume, resumable_note, how_long, memory, last_run, plan_lines, others_pending, name_facts, field_facts) -> tuple[Fact, ...]`,** in 8.4's order:
  - `uses`: `Fact("info", "Model Nadrowski; bounds file master_tier1.txt; prior retrain_prior; frequency response with 12 slots; rotation on.")`.
  - `budget`: `Fact("info", "10,000 batches of 2,048 make 20,480,000 simulations. Each simulates one spontaneous recording and 2 to 12 driven recordings.")`; for a spontaneous kind "Each simulates one spontaneous recording."; forced "Each simulates one spontaneous recording and one driven recording."
  - Continue or new: "Fine. Continues the saved simulation set: 3,989 of 10,000 batches are done." / "Fine. No simulations this time: the saved 20,480,000 are used." / "Information. Starts a new simulation set; none is saved for this model yet." / "Attention. Starts a new simulation set. 1 saved set for this model does not match these settings:" then every difference (`fields.difference_lines`, Task 11 gives the full table; Task 10 uses the keys it can name and the raw key else) / with "Never": "Attention. The simulations of this training are not saved, so no saved simulation set is continued." `checkpoint_error`: "Attention. The saved simulation sets could not be read: <error>."
  - Departure 114 by 130's words, each `info`: "The fit is saved after every epoch, so a stop or a failure keeps it." and "A resume first reads the simulation set back, about four minutes at full size."; with "Never" one `warn`: "A training whose simulation set is not kept cannot be resumed: a stop or a failure during the fit loses it."
  - How long: "Simulation: about 28 hours: the last training of this size, retrain, took 9.7 s per batch. Fit: about 130 epochs of about 63 minutes." (`history.estimate`'s own source words); with neither: "No time estimate: no training of this size has run on this computer, and the timing of a short fit has not been run."
  - Memory: `limit_words(force)`, then `ends.last_run_sentence`, led by `info`.
  - `others_pending`: "Step 2, Prior, has changes that are not applied. This run uses what is in use: retrain_prior." as `warn`.
  - "Only continue" with nothing to continue: `Fact("err", "There is no saved simulation set to continue.", "there is nothing to continue")`; "Only start new" with one: `Fact("err", "A saved simulation set would be continued.", "a saved simulation set would be continued")`. No prior: `Fact("lock", "Needs a prior: build or load one in step 2, Prior.", "needs a prior")`.
- **The start.** `dispatch(orchestrator.build_posterior, s.cfg, s.inf_prior, None, True, name=, note=, num_runs=, run_size_cap=, hidden_features=, num_transforms=, learning_rate=, stop_after_epochs=, checkpoint_every=, resume=, new_run=, if_name_taken="unnamed", provide_fig_sink=True, on_result=self._on_posterior)`, with `max_num_epochs` only when a limit is typed and the three rotation settings only when used. `run_spec` calls `training_spec(..., expected_epochs=<typed>, plan=self._screen.plan_entry(), settings=self.settings_entry())`. A second press finds the button locked by the run's start, and the name check at the press; a press while a run is live starts nothing. After `dispatch` answers True: `put_many` of `inference_posterior/num_runs`, `run_size_cap`, as text.

- **`take_posterior(loaded)`:** `s.reset_downstream("posterior")`, `s.posterior = loaded`, `put("inference_posterior/posterior", loaded.id)`, `refresh_gates()` once. Today's old posterior stays in `session` through the run; the exit's card test reads what that costs.

Saved as a record." from `manifest.body["training"]`; past a set limit, "The fit reached the epoch limit; the network kept is its last epoch's."; the "Training loss" figure; "Go to Checks". A record back with no name: Task 9's sentence.

## Task 11: Step 3: the load view and the questions

**Corrected by the trial (departures 247 to 257) and its fix round (258 to 262):** the rules and commits below stood before it. Carried-over rows: the replacements of the rows Task 10 closed (`tests/test_nav_and_gating.py:439`, `:1224`, `:1292`, `:3589`'s load leg, `:4582`'s posterior half; `tests/test_settings_persistence.py:831`); `tests/test_settings_persistence.py:793` (kept).

**Files:**
- Modify: `core/gui/panels/inference/posterior_tab.py`, `core/gui/fields.py`, `core/gui/step_facts.py`, the suites above; built, also `core/gui/style.py` (247), `core/gui/widgets/artifact_picker.py` (254), `core/gui/panels/inference/prior_tab.py` (`recorded_file`), `core/gui/remembered.py` (`POSTERIOR_KEY`'s comment), `core/gui/run/controller.py` (a docstring wrapped), `tests/_dialogs.py` (`held_enter`), `tests/_fixtures.py` (`_posterior_artifact`'s `parents`, `readings_of_a_run`), `tests/test_applied_and_pending.py`, `tests/test_before_you_start.py`, `tests/test_look.py`, `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py`, `tests/test_pages.py`, `tests/test_run_states.py`, `tests/test_shell.py`, `tests/test_step_chain.py` (each view of the Training page pressed)

**Interfaces:** Produces `load_facts(*, blocked, trained_with, in_use, narrowed, facts_of_record, plan_lines=()) -> tuple[Fact, ...]`, `IDENTITY_ROWS`, `difference_lines`, `PosteriorPanel.load_record(ref) -> bool`. Restores `post_picker`, `_on_posterior`. Consumes `orchestrator.matching_fits`, `training_reading`, `ask(items=…)`.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **Load view.** Heading's button: "Use a saved posterior instead…" / "Set up a training instead…". "Saved posterior" (`StorePicker("posterior", keeps_selection=True)`), facts under it from its record: kind of observation, network size, simulations, the prior it was trained with, the bounds file, "from a narrowing round" where `body["amortized"]` is false. `load_facts`: "Fine. Trained with retrain_prior, the prior in use." or "Attention. Trained with retrain_prior; the prior in use is other_prior. Calibration and a narrowing round will refuse this pair. Load retrain_prior in step 2 first." "Needs a prior" blocks both views (departure 133). Selection row: "Saved posterior". "Load this posterior": `dispatch(orchestrator.build_posterior, s.cfg, s.inf_prior, ref, False, accept=accept, on_result=self._on_posterior)`. A narrowed record first asks 8.4's question, word for word, its "Then:" sentence as `beneath`: "Cancel" safe; "Load this posterior" other, passing `Accept(truncated=True)`. The observation's name from the record `parents["observation"]` names. No on-screen string of a rebuilt page names the Prior, Posterior, Infer, TSNPE or Validate tab. `load_record(ref)` is this with the picker set, for the chain. `take_posterior` gains its picker here: it is re-listed and moved at the page's `run_ended`, not in the result handler.
- **`IDENTITY_ROWS`, `difference_lines`:** 8.7's table as data. Lists are compared entry by entry; only entries that differ are shown, by parameter name, uncut: "Bounds file: limits of K_gs: 0.1 to 1 here, 0.2 to 1 in this set". `param_keys` differing: "Bounds file: parameters", the two lists. `units_sha256` and the three time exponents fold into one "Units" line. The four time-grid entries fold into "Time grid", each entry's value per side. `format`, `summary_flags`, `feature_set_version`, `chi_layout`, `chi_elem_w` fold into "Made by another version of PRISM". `truncation`: "Made by a narrowing round: a training never continues it." `device`, `dtype`: "This computer: made on the graphics card (or the processor)". `prior_fingerprint`: each side's label through `store.find_prior_by_fingerprint`, else "a prior no longer in Records". `model` differing: the set is not listed.
- **The from-zero question,** after the fields, the name's check and one more reading: asked when the reading says the run would not continue its own set and `sibling_diffs` holds a set of this model. Title "Start a new simulation set?"; "This training will simulate 10,000 batches from zero. Saved simulation sets for this model exist, and none matches these settings."; items, for each set, "Simulation set for retrain_prior (2 Oct 2026, 09:10; 10,000 batches, complete) differs in:" then one line per difference; beneath "To continue one of them, cancel and set these back."; "Cancel" safe; "Start training on a new simulation set" other, passing `new_run=True`. With "Never": sets read at the built-in cadence; a set that reading would continue heads the list with "No setting differs. It is not continued because the simulations of this training are not saved."; the text ends "…exist, and none will be continued."; the other answer reads "Start training without saving the simulations".
- **The choice (fit-saving 4.4):** the shared question has two answers, so the choice is that question and the Training step's list. When `matching_fits(...)` holds any: title "Start a new training?"; "1 kept training matches these settings and can be resumed:"; items, each "<label>: <how it stopped>, after epoch 41, saved 3 Oct 2026, 03:12"; beneath "A new training leaves it as it is. To resume it, cancel and press Resume beside it on this page."; "Cancel" safe; "Start a new training" other. A match implies a complete set, so this and the from-zero question are never both asked; the code asks the choice first and returns. The list it names is built in Task 14.
- **Order at the click:** fields; name; one more reading; the choice or the from-zero question; `dispatch`. A refusal from the stage with key `new_run` (`_refusal` override): read again and ask the from-zero question; a yes dispatches again with `new_run=True`; the engine's sentence is not shown. A refusal after a match is possible: the page re-lists.

**Interfaces:** Produces R-plans, and fills `InferenceScreen.plan_entry()`. Consumes `engine_plans.read_plan`, `plan_text`, `HEADINGS`, `ALLOWED`; `StepPage.plan_fields()`; `provenance.file_ref`; `cli.units_to_factors`; `ask`, `ask_text`; `remembered.put_many`.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **The foot follows the plan in use** (departure 196). On "Model and options" `reads_the_disk` is a property, True while a plan is in use: the plan's file is read, so the "read at" line and "Check again" come back with a plan and go with "No plan", at the next drawing.
- **Where it stands.** A block on "Model and options" directly under "Model": `plan_choice`, `class _PlanChoice(Choice)`, holds "No plan" then the model's plans by stem; `showPopup` re-reads the folder; every `.txt` is listed, a hand-named `retrain v2.txt` too. The list follows the model shown on the form, and a change of it returns to "No plan" with no plan in use, values staying. At launch "No plan", never stored.
- **The block:** every row of 7.1.6's table, word for word. "Show what it holds" is a fold over a `RuleTable` of step, setting ("Batches (num_runs)"), the plan's value, the usual value; later headings' rows read "not applied yet", the count holding them (departure 141). No button is primary. "Open the plans folder" is `QDesktopServices.openUrl`, greyed while the model's folder does not exist. Locked by a run with the form.
- **The window's two checks,** in `read_checked`: "The bounds file master_tier1.txt is not in this model's bounds folder." and "The units are not usable: <reason>", shown with the reader's problems as one list; nothing is filled.
- **`fill_forms(screen, values, source, keep_remembered=False)`** asks nothing, starts nothing, applies nothing, calls no `new_draft`, changes no record's pick. For each page's `plan_fields()`, by `HEADING_OF[page.step]`: the source's value, else `row.usual()`. `keep_remembered`: a row marked kept from last time is left as it is, and reads "from plan retrain" or "changed from the plan" where the plan holds it. `bounds`: left alone when the plan names none; with one, and the model on the form applied, it selects the file; with the model not applied, `prior_panel.pending_bounds(name)` holds it, and `on_draft_set` selects it by name in the new model's folder (the stash made public; a name not there: the sentence of Task 9). The source is kept on `InferenceScreen.plan_source` as `PlanSource`, which outlives `new_draft`.
- **The picker's press:** `read_checked`; `would_replace` lists values neither usual nor the source's; if any, `ask`: "Fill the forms from plan retrain?", "Filling from plan retrain sets every setting a plan can hold: 14 from the plan, the rest to their usual values.", items "Batches: 9,500 becomes 10,000", "Keep what is there", "Fill the forms". The usual-values press: "Return the forms to the usual values?", "This sets every setting a plan can hold to its usual value.", "Keep what is there", "Return to the usual values". Steps 1 to 3 only.
- **Origins.** `plans.origin(row, source, *, kept)` is `origin_words` on the row's values; `StepPage` sets each row's origin after every edit, a fill and an apply. "kept from last time" is a flag on the row, cleared by its first touch. A row's origin at Apply is kept with the applied value for `settings_entry()`, whose `plan` field fills from here.
- **`plan_facts(screen, headings)`,** 7.1.9's sentences exact, each `info` but the changed file's `warn`, every departure named: the settings the run reads, applied values of earlier steps, form values of its own. The file's hash is read through a cache keyed by modification time and size. "Settings from record …", "Settings from run …" for those sources. The Prior page passes `("Model and options", "Prior")`, Training the three.
- **The plan line,** a quiet line under each of the three headings: "Plan: retrain", "Plan: retrain, 2 changes", "No plan".
- **Save:** `ask_text(self, "Save as a plan", "Only the settings that differ from their usual values are written. A setting left out follows the usual value.", "Plan name", "Save", check=<the rule>, items=<what will be written>, beneath=<typed bounds' and changed rows' sentences>)`. The rule: `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,40}$`, else "A plan's name is letters, digits, _ and -, at most 40 characters." Written: every setting of 7.1.3 whose form value differs from its usual, the bounds file when one is selected, each with its label as a remark; the three driven-recording settings only with `chi_mode = on`. "The bounds were typed by hand, so this plan names no bounds file." Nothing to write: the button greyed, "Every setting is at its usual value, so there is nothing to save." A taken name: `ask`, "Replace plan retrain?", "retrain.txt was last changed on 3 Oct 2026, 14:02. The earlier file cannot be brought back.", "Keep the existing plan", "Replace it", `loses_work=True`. `save_plan` writes a sibling temporary file and `os.replace`s it. The saved plan becomes the source in use; no value changes. The page then says "Saved as plan retrain. Commit this file before a long run, or the run's records are marked as made from a changed tree."
- **History:** `plan_entry()`: None with no source; for a plan `{"kind": "plan", "name", "path", "sha256", "filled_sha256", "filled_at", "changed"}` from `provenance.file_ref(path, relative_to=config.RESOURCES_ROOT, missing_ok=True)`, so a file removed since the fill stops nothing; for a record or a run its kind, id and name.
- **Last used:** `plans/last_used/<model>` and `/last_used_at/<model>`, the model lower-cased, join the `put_many` of `new_draft`, `take_prior`, `take_posterior` and the Training start; blank clears the hint.
- **`ask.py`:** the sentence that says what is wrong with a typed line wraps to the question's width (691.2) and takes its line's height from the start (691.6); the dialog's record keeps the label, the line handed in and what is wrong (695.4); 695.2's tests.
- **Tests' folder.** `plans.PLAN_FOLDER`; `_no_plans_written` rebinds it to a temporary folder for the session; `build_inference(plans=…)` hands the screen its own. `process_state` holds it.

**Implements:** U24, B16, C1; 3.6's rows for the picks; `remembered_picks`, 7.3.1 rule 2; `LoadChain` as probe 4 found it; `InferenceScreen.load_chain` (8.1), for the chain's model, options, bounds file, prior and posterior. Carried-over rows: `tests/test_step_chain.py:706`, `:840`; `tests/test_shell.py:1160`.

**Files:**
- Create: `core/gui/load_chain.py`
- Modify: `core/gui/remembered.py`, `core/gui/chain.py`, `core/gui/screens/home_screen.py`, `core/gui/main_window.py` (`_home_facts`, `_stored`), `core/gui/screens/inference_screen.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/config_tab.py`, `tests/test_step_chain.py`, `tests/test_shell.py`, `tests/test_run_states.py`, `tests/test_settings_persistence.py`; built, also `core/gui/panels/inference/prior_tab.py` and `tests/test_plans.py` (the first commit), and `main_window.py`'s close (295)

**Interfaces:** Produces R-chain, `ConfigPanel.apply_quietly(model, options)`, `Picks`, `remembered_picks`, `Next.action`, `Next.link`, `HomeFacts.picks`, `chain.last_time_sentence(picks) -> str`, `HomeScreen.action_pressed`. Consumes `fill_forms`, `PriorPanel.load_record`, `PosteriorPanel.load_record`, `controller().run_ended`.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **`remembered_picks(*, store)`** reads `inference_config/model`, `inference_config/chi_mode`, `inference_prior/bounds`, `inference_prior/prior`, `inference_posterior/posterior`, `plans/last_used/<model>`. Each is left out (None) when its file or record is gone: `store.get`, a `Path` check under `BOUNDS_PATH`, `read_checked` for the plan. `Picks` also holds `prior_label`, `posterior_label`. `prior` stays only while `bounds` stands; `posterior` only while `prior` stays and its record's `parents["prior"]` is that prior. No model: nothing to offer. The first launch after this stage reads keys the old build wrote at closing: said in the exit's rulings, no code.
- **The row.** After the run rows, when `s.draft` is None and `picks` names a model: `Next("Next: continue with what was in use last time", <sentence>, "Continue with these", "home", None, action="continue_with_these")`. The sentence, exact, of what the press applies and loads: "Last time: the model Nadrowski, the frequency response as the kind of observation, the bounds file master_tier1.txt, the prior retrain_prior, the posterior retrain, the plan retrain. Nothing is loaded until you press the button." ("the kind of observation set by the bounds file"; each item gone is left out.) `chain.first_time` does not change.
- **`_stored`** gains `picks` as its third entry: one reading per `_stored`. While a run is live the third entry keeps the last reading (None at a first), as the fourth does (Task 14). `_home_facts` adds nothing to a status change.
- **`LoadChain`:** `start(links)` connects `controller().run_ended` to a bound method and starts the first; a link returning False stops with "a run is live", and the page says "A run is live. Only one runs at a time."; `run_ended` with `outcome == "finished"` starts the next by `QTimer.singleShot(0, self, self._next)`, never directly; any other outcome stops with that outcome, or "refused" for None; it disconnects, and `stopped` and `finished` go by `QTimer.singleShot(0, self, …)`, so a first link refused at once is still heard by a caller that connects to the chain `load_chain` returned. `stopped` can fire while a refusal's box stands open: nothing hangs on the box's answer.
- **`load_chain(model, options, bounds_file, prior, posterior=None, observation=None)`:** `config_panel.apply_quietly(model, options)` (sets the form, `_build_config`'s reading and `new_draft`, no question: the caller asked first, or nothing is held), the bounds pick selected, then the links `prior_panel.load_record(prior)` and, with a posterior, `posterior_panel.load_record(posterior)`; returns the started chain. A narrowed posterior's question is asked inside its link.
- **Options:** `{"chi_mode": picks.chi_mode}` and the plan's Model-and-options values, nothing else: every other option at its built-in value.
- **The press.** `_next_pressed` emits `action_pressed(action)` when `Next.action` is set. `MainWindow._action("continue_with_these")`: wait cursor, in a `try/finally`; `self.part("inference")`, which leaves Home in sight and `_inference_opened` False; `fill_forms(..., keep_remembered=True)` when the plan stands; `load_chain`; on `finished` and `stopped` `_stored = None` and Home refreshed. The person stays on Home. A press during a live run cannot happen: the row is not shown. The foot's "Loading…" between links is left as it is (probe 4: one frame, to be read on the real screen in G11).

## Task 14: After a reopening: Continue and Resume

**Implements:** R-continuing; 6.8 with departures 136 to 140; fit-saving 4.2, 5.3, 5.6, 6.2; its last review's stage-4 notes; C2, C4, D6, D8; departures 97, 110's words; Review Focus 2; the head's card paragraph. Carried-over rows: `tests/test_run_text.py:795`, `tests/test_stop_and_close.py:1369` (`continuable`, its line `:1434`), `tests/test_step_chain.py:51`, `:61`, `:617`, `:679`, `:706`, `tests/test_look.py:747` (`FIXED_FACE_CALLERS`, exact at `:841`, gains `posterior_tab.py` for the list's command), `:6681`, `tests/test_run_states.py:4333` (`:4551`: the command no longer shows for a Training), `tests/test_shell.py:1808` (stands: a stopped training still reads its batch), `tests/test_retry.py`'s reopen sentences, `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py:1480` (`resume_fit`).

**Files:**
- Create: `core/gui/continuing.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/resume.py`
- Modify: `core/gui/chain.py`, `core/gui/screens/home_screen.py`, `core/gui/screens/inference_screen.py`, `core/gui/main_window.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/posterior_tab.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/prior_tab.py` (`load_record`'s candidate), `core/gui/run/controller.py`, `core/gui/run/history.py`, `core/gui/run/retry.py`, `core/gui/run/view.py`, `core/gui/fields.py`, `tests/test_retry.py`, `tests/test_run_text.py`, `tests/test_run_states.py`, `tests/test_stop_and_close.py`, `tests/test_step_chain.py`, `tests/test_shell.py`, `tests/test_look.py`, `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py`, `tests/test_gpu_paths.py`

**Interfaces:** Produces R-continuing, R-home's second half, `InferenceScreen.offers() -> tuple[Offer, ...]`, `InferenceScreen.read_offers() -> None`, `PosteriorPanel.resume(kept) -> bool`, `PosteriorPanel.continue_run(offer) -> None`. Consumes `fit_state.kept_fits`, `KeptFit`'s new fields, `history.read`, `fill_forms`, `load_chain`, `LoadChain`, `store.delete("fit", id)`, `orchestrator.build_posterior`.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **`offers(histories, kept_rows, *, records_root, set_batches)`,** pure, newest first. `set_batches(folder)` is `peek`'s count, None for no set: the saved batch is the set's own count, read from the set. `"resume"`: a kept row of no region, `can_resume`, not running; its `how` "stopped unexpectedly" or "kept when stopped" (departure 136: both; a tool's record too); `"round"`: a row with a region, carrying `command = round_command(kept)` (departure 137); a kept row that cannot be resumed: its `why_not` filled, no Home row. `"continue"`: the newest history of the word "Training", of `records_root`, outcome "failed" or "not seen to end", whose set is unfinished (`set_batches(folder)` below `simulation_set["batches"]`, above 0), `settings` not None, no later run of that word started. `"fit_again"`: the same with the set complete and no resumable kept training on it: `"Fit again on the saved simulations"` (departure 140). `"stopped"`: the newest Training history stopped with its set unfinished and no later run. A history with no `settings`, or one whose `settings` names no bounds file, makes no continue offer (departure 139). A narrowing round's history makes no continue offer at this stage: its page is not rebuilt and a fill does not reach it (as departure 137 has it for a kept round): Home says how it ended.
- **`read_offers(store, folder)`:** `store.list("fit")`, empty: no `kept_fits` call; else `kept_fits(store)`, on the window's thread (probe 5), plus the history files of `records_root`; never while a run is live: a reading asked then keeps the last, and a first reading reads nothing. Triggers: Home's read (`_stored`'s fourth entry), a run's end, a store change, each Resume or Delete. `InferenceScreen.read_offers` asks it and calls `recompute_states`; `MainWindow._home_facts` uses the screen's when it is built, its own read otherwise.
- **The chain.** `step_states(kept=<newest offer>)` replaces `ended`: "resume": detail "Training kept after epoch 41" (`Offer.epoch`), rail "Unfinished: epoch 41"; "continue" and "stopped": "Simulations saved to batch 236", "Unfinished: batch 236", in this launch and after a reopening; marker `half`, `can_start` True, standing before the rule that a step needs a prior (a Resume needs none in the session). A "fit_again" or "round" offer changes no row. `controller().training_kept`, `_training_kept` go.
- **`next_step`,** in 3.6's order, with `offers`: the unseen-end row: failed or not seen to end with an offer: the offer's button, `link="See what happened"`. Then, the end seen, the newest offer: "continue": "Next: continue the training", when it failed and what is kept, "Continue from batch 236", `action="continue_run"`; "fit_again": the same with "Fit again on the saved simulations"; "resume": heading "Next: resume the training retrain_3", sentence `offer.sentence`, made by `continuing.resume_sentence(kept)`: "It stopped unexpectedly after epoch 41, last saved Sat 3 Oct, 03:12. Best validation loss -3.10, 2 epochs ago. It trains on the simulation set eda5c2a1e468 and the prior retrain_prior. Resuming first reads the simulation set back, about four minutes at full size." ("It was kept when stopped…"), button "Resume", `action="resume"`. A "round" offer: heading "Next: resume the narrowing round", the sentence "It is resumed from the command line until its page offers it: " and its command, button "Open Training", which opens the step whose list shows the command (departure 137). A "stopped" offer is passed over, so Home stays ordinary after a stop (`03-shell.md:478`). These stand above "Continue with these".
- **The press of Continue** (`_action("continue_run")`): the page opens at the run's own step. `fill_forms(screen, values, PlanSource("run", <id>, …))`, values from the history's `settings["rows"]`; `load_chain(settings["model"], settings["options"], settings["bounds"], settings["prior"])`; on `finished`, `posterior_panel.continue_run(offer)`: `resume_choice` at "Only continue", Name and Note blank, `set_hint("The earlier run was named retrain_3.")`, the main button "Continue from batch 236", N the reading's own `batches_done`, or "Fit again on the saved simulations". The start needs a name typed and asks nothing: `resume="require"`. No consent is replayed. Something held: `ask`, title "Continue from batch 236?", naming what it lets go by `session_contents()`, "Keep this session", "Continue and start a new session".
- **`resume_problems(kept, *, device, store)`,** before anything is read, each a sentence: bounds file gone ("The bounds file master.txt is no longer there."); its `sha256` differs ("The bounds file master.txt has changed since this training began."); typed limits with a forcing section ("This training was started from limits typed by hand with a forcing section, which its record cannot rebuild."); units file gone or changed, in the same words; prior gone ("The prior it was trained with is no longer in Records."); `kept.config["device"]` against `device.device.type`, or `run_size` above `device.batch_size` ("It was trained on the graphics card; this computer has none now."). A library or code change is `why_not`.
- **`resume_draft(kept) -> ConfigDraft`, `resume_config(kept)`:** from `kept.inputs`, `kept.config` (`mode`, `chi_mode`, `chi_k_pad`, `chi_max_cycles`, `reparam_rotate`, `state_dep_drift`, `units`), never a form. `chi_n_freqs` takes its built-in: it enters no identity. `resume_call(kept)`: `{"resume_fit": kept.id}`, and `name` when asked for. It never sends the budget, flow, patience, ceiling, cadence or note, `save_fit`, `resume`.
- **The press of Resume** (Home, the Training step's list, a past view, the same path): `resume_problems`: any, said on the page, nothing read. A name: `kept.posterior_name`, else `ask_text(self, "Name this training", "This kept training has no name yet. A posterior needs one.", "Name", "Resume", check=<the name's check>)`; Cancel starts nothing. Something held: `ask`, "Resume this training?", naming what it lets go, "Keep this session", "Resume and start a new session". Then `new_draft(resume_draft(kept))`, `prior_panel.load_record(kept.parents["prior"], candidate=resume_config(kept))`, on `finished` `posterior_panel.resume(kept)`: `dispatch(orchestrator.build_posterior, s.cfg, s.inf_prior, None, True, **resume_call(kept), if_name_taken="unnamed", provide_fig_sink=True, on_result=self._on_posterior)`. `run_spec` takes patience, epoch limit, network and cadence from `kept.config`, `spec.name` the kept name. A state "finished": the sentence says "Its fit is finished; resuming writes the posterior without training again."
- **The Training step's list,** in region 2, read once at the triggers, never live; a run's own record reads "running" and offers nothing. One row each: its label, then `resume_sentence`, which holds what 4.2 asks a row to show, then "Resume" and "Delete". Cannot be resumed: `why_not`, "Delete" alone. A round: its command in the fixed face, selectable, a Tab stop. "Delete": `ask`, "Delete this kept training?", "The 41 epochs kept are deleted; the next training starts from its first epoch.", "Keep it", "Delete it", `loses_work=True`; `store.delete("fit", id)`; the list re-read. A refusal at a Resume (a race, a torn record, a process training it): its sentence on the row, with "Delete", and the list re-read. A kept training whose form values differ is still listed; the row says "It uses the settings it was kept with."
- **`CONTROL["resume_fit"]`:** `f"Look at the kept trainings {_in('training')}."`
- **The past view.** `view.offer_past(offer)`: on a reopened run the buttons of 6.8 and Discard, which `controller.discard` already serves; a press emits `controller().offer_pressed(offer)`, connected to a bound method of the window, the same path as Home.
- **Words.** `retry._reopening` (departures 97, 110): batches: "<opening> Close PRISM and open it again; Home will offer 'Continue from batch 236'."; kept in the fit: "<opening> Close PRISM and open it again; Home and the Training step will offer 'Resume the training'."; a narrowing round's: today's, with its command. `reopen_command` answers "" for "Training". `command_line` and `failure_message` take `Qt.StrongFocus` while they show, and the ring.
- **`history.continuable` goes,** with `tests/test_run_text.py:795`, its cases carried into `tests/test_retry.py`; `tests/test_stop_and_close.py:1434`, one line of a test that stays, asks `continuing.offers` instead: a run shut down by Windows offers no Continue.
